The cylinder rotates inside it.\n#   As cylinder rotates, needle butts (extending outward through cylinder wall)\n#   travel along the cam track on the inner surface of this ring.\n#   The cam profile lifts and lowers needles as they pass through the knit zone.\n#\n# CAM PROFILE (V-cam, single knit point):\n#   - REST zone (315 deg): butt at low Z, no lift\n#   - RISE zone (22.5 deg): smooth ramp up\n#   - PEAK zone (5 deg): butt at maximum height (yarn pickup window)\n#   - FALL zone (17.5 deg): smooth ramp down (latch closes, stitch forms)\n#\n# NEEDLE BUTT TRAVEL:\n#   Rest position: butt center at Z ~21mm in cylinder\n#   Lift amount:   ~10mm (sock knitting standard)\n#   Peak position: butt center at Z ~31mm\n#\n# CAUTION:\n#   Cam profile is the most uncertain part of the design.\n#   This V6 uses CONSERVATIVE defaults for first build.\n#   EXPECT iteration after first knit test (V7+).\n# =============================================================================\n\nimport = os.path.join(BASE, \"blueprints\", \"CamRing_V6_Blueprint.svg\")\n\nfor p in [PATH_STL_FULL, PATH_STL_SLICE, PATH_PNG_FULL, PATH_SVG]:\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(p), exist_ok=True)\n\n#      # cam track ends 19mm above ring bottom (15mm tall track)\nTRACK_HEIGHT     = TRACK_Z_TOP - TRACK_Z_BOT  # 15mm\n\n# Cam profile - depths cut into wall (radial)\nTRACK_DEPTH_REST = 3.0       # shallow cut at rest zone (butt sits in this much)\nTRACK_DEPTH_PEAK = 9.0       # deep cut at peak (forces butt to lift)\n# These translate to Z-position of needle:\n# Rest depth 3mm -> butt center at low Z position\n# Peak depth 9mm -> butt center at high Z position\n# Difference: 6mm of needle lift from cam action\n\n# Cam profile - angular zones\nANGLE_REST_END   = 270.0     # rest zone: 0 to 6\nPIN_PCD          = 145.0     # alignment pins for cassette stack\nPIN_HOLE_D       = 4.10      # 4mm dowel + 0.10mm clearance\nPIN_SLOT_LEN     = 4.50      # slotted holes (locator + 5 orientators)\n\nBOLT_COUNT       = 6         # mount bolts to cassette base\nBOLT_PCD         = 155.0\nBOLT_HOLE_D      = 5.4       # M5 clearance\nBOLT_CB_D        = 9.5       # counterbore\nBOLT_CB_H        = 5.0\n\n# Yarn entry clearance (above ring, between needle hooks and feeder)\n# This is just visual marker - actual yarn path is above ring\nFEEDER_POSITIONS = [0, 60, 120, 180, 240, 300]  # 6 feeders at 60deg\n\nEDGE_CHAMFER     = 0.8\n\n# =============================================================================\n# DERIVED VALUES\n# =============================================================================\nRING_OUTER_R     = RING_OD / 2.0\nRING_INNER_R     = RING_ID / 2.0\nTRACK_INNER_R_REST = RING_INNER_R + TRACK_DEPTH_REST  # at rest, track inner edge\nTRACK_INNER_R_PEAK = RING_INNER_R + TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK  # at peak\n\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\nprint(\"CSM V3 - CAM RING V6 (Legare 4.5\\\", single V-cam)\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\nprint(f\"  Ring OD:          {RING_OD}mm\")\nprint(f\"  Ring ID:          {RING_ID}mm (clears {RING_ID - 0.7}mm cylinder)\")\nprint(f\"  Ring height:      {RING_HEIGHT}mm\")\nprint(f\"  Wall thickness:   {WALL_THICKNESS}mm\")\nprint(f\"  Cam track Z:      {TRACK_Z_BOT}-{TRACK_Z_TOP}mm\")\nprint(f\"  Cam profile:\")\nprint(f\"    Rest depth:     {TRACK_DEPTH_REST}mm (315 deg of ring)\")\nprint(f\"    Peak depth:     {TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK}mm (5 deg, at ~295 deg)\")\nprint(f\"    Lift amount:    {TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK - TRACK_DEPTH_REST}mm needle travel\")\nprint(f\"  Knit zone:        45 deg (rise + peak + fall)\")\nprint(f\"  Mount: {BOLT_COUNT}x M5 @ PCD {BOLT_PCD}mm\")\nprint(f\"  Pins:  {PIN_COUNT} alignment @ PCD {PIN_PCD}mm\")\nprint()\n\n# FreeCAD.newDocument(\"CSM_CamRing_V6\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BUILD: BASE RING\n# =============================================================================\nprint(\"[1/3] Building base ring...\")\n\n# Hollow cylinder (outer minus inner)\nouter_cyl = Part.makeCylinder(RING_OUTER_R, RING_HEIGHT)\ninner_cyl = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R, RING_HEIGHT + 2)\ninner_cyl.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\nring_body = outer_cyl.cut(inner_cyl)\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BUILD: CAM TRACK (the inner annular groove with V-cam profile)\n# =============================================================================\nprint(\"[2/3] Building cam track (V-cam profile)...\")\n\n# Strategy: cut the cam track as multiple boolean operations.\n# Step 1: cut a uniform rest-depth track (3mm radial) all the way around\n#         from Z=TRACK_Z_BOT to Z=TRACK_Z_TOP\n# Step 2: cut deeper segments at the rise/peak/fall zones using rotated boxes\n\n# Step 1: Uniform rest track around the entire ring\n# This is a flat annular cut at depth TRACK_DEPTH_REST\nrest_outer = Part.makeCylinder(RING_OUTER_R + 1, TRACK_HEIGHT,\n                               FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, TRACK_Z_BOT))\nrest_inner_radius = RING_INNER_R  # don't cut anything inside the ID\nrest_inner = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R, TRACK_HEIGHT + 0.1,\n                              FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, TRACK_Z_BOT - 0.05))\n# Actually we want to remove material from RING_INNER_R outward by TRACK_DEPTH_REST\n# So the cut is between RING_INNER_R and RING_INNER_R + TRACK_DEPTH_REST\nrest_track_cutter_outer = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R + TRACK_DEPTH_REST,\n                                            TRACK_HEIGHT,\n                                            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, TRACK_Z_BOT))\nrest_track_cutter_inner = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R,\n                                            TRACK_HEIGHT + 0.1,\n                                            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, TRACK_Z_BOT - 0.05))\nrest_track_cutter = rest_track_cutter_outer.cut(rest_track_cutter_inner)\nring_body = ring_body.cut(rest_track_cutter)\nprint(f\"  Rest track cut at depth {TRACK_DEPTH_REST}mm, full circumference\")\n\n# Step 2: Deeper cuts at rise, peak, and fall zones\n# We approximate the V-cam profile with 3 radial cuts that vary in depth.\n# At the peak, we cut deeper into the wall (TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK).\n# Rise and fall zones are at intermediate depth.\n\n# For simplicity in V1.0, use stepped depths:\n# Rise zone (270-292.5): cut to depth 6mm (intermediate)\n# Peak zone (292.5-297.5): cut to depth 9mm (max)\n# Fall zone (297.5-315): cut to depth 6mm (intermediate)\n\ndef cut_angular_segment(ring, angle_start_deg, angle_end_deg, depth_mm,\n                        z_bot, z_height):\n    \"\"\"Cut an angular segment of the inner wall to the specified depth.\"\"\"\n    angle_span = angle_end_deg - angle_start_deg\n    # Build a partial cylinder spanning the angular range\n    # FreeCAD makeCylinder with angle parameter creates a sector\n    sector = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R + depth_mm, z_height,\n                              FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, z_bot),\n                              FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\n                              angle_span)\n    # Rotate to start angle\n    sector.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0),\n                  FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\n                  angle_start_deg)\n    # Subtract the inner part (we don't want to cut beyond ID)\n    inner_keep = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R, z_height + 0.1,\n                                  FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, z_bot - 0.05))\n    sector_cut = sector.cut(inner_keep)\n    return ring.cut(sector_cut)\n\n# Apply rise zone (intermediate depth)\nring_body = cut_angular_segment(ring_body,\n                                ANGLE_REST_END, ANGLE_RISE_END,\n                                (TRACK_DEPTH_REST + TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK) / 2.0,\n                                TRACK_Z_BOT, TRACK_HEIGHT)\nprint(f\"  Rise zone: {ANGLE_REST_END}-{ANGLE_RISE_END} deg, intermediate depth\")\n\n# Apply peak zone (max depth)\nring_body = cut_angular_segment(ring_body,\n                                ANGLE_RISE_END, ANGLE_PEAK_END,\n                                TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK,\n                                TRACK_Z_BOT, TRACK_HEIGHT)\nprint(f\"  Peak zone: {ANGLE_RISE_END}-{ANGLE_PEAK_END} deg, max depth\")\n\n# Apply fall zone (intermediate depth)\nring_body = cut_angular_segment(ring_body,\n                                ANGLE_PEAK_END, ANGLE_FALL_END,\n                                (TRACK_DEPTH_REST + TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK) / 2.0,\n                                TRACK_Z_BOT, TRACK_HEIGHT)\nprint(f\"  Fall zone: {ANGLE_PEAK_END}-{ANGLE_FALL_END} deg, intermediate depth\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BUILD: ALIGNMENT PIN HOLES\n# =============================================================================\nprint(\"[3/3] Cutting pin and bolt holes...\")\n\n# 6 pin holes at PCD 145mm (60 deg spacing)\n# Hole 1 (0 deg): round locator\n# Holes 2-6: slotted (tangential slots for thermal/print compensation)\nfor i in range(PIN_COUNT):\n    angle = i * (360.0 / PIN_COUNT)\n    rad = PIN_PCD / 2.0\n    x = rad * math.cos(math.radians(angle))\n    y = rad * math.sin(math.radians(angle))\n\n    if i == 0:\n        # Locator: round hole\n        hole = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, RING_HEIGHT + 2)\n        hole.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x, y, -1))\n    else:\n        # Orientator: slotted hole (tangential extension)\n        # Build by fusing two holes offset tangentially\n        slot_offset = (PIN_SLOT_LEN - PIN_HOLE_D) / 2.0\n        # Tangential direction at this angle\n        tan_x = -math.sin(math.radians(angle)) * slot_offset\n        tan_y =  math.cos(math.radians(angle)) * slot_offset\n\n        hole_a = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, RING_HEIGHT + 2)\n        hole_a.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x + tan_x, y + tan_y, -1))\n\n        hole_b = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, RING_HEIGHT + 2)\n        hole_b.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x - tan_x, y - tan_y, -1))\n\n        # Connect with rectangular section\n        hole_a = hole_a.fuse(hole_b)\n        hole = hole_a\n\n    ring_body = ring_body.cut(hole)\n\n# 6 bolt holes at PCD 155mm (60 deg spacing, between pins)\nfor i in range(BOLT_COUNT):\n    angle = i * (360.0 / BOLT_COUNT) + 30.0  # offset 30 deg between pins\n    rad = BOLT_PCD / 2.0\n    x = rad * math.cos(math.radians(angle))\n    y = rad * math.sin(math.radians(angle))\n\n    # M5 clearance hole\n    hole = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_HOLE_D / 2.0, RING_HEIGHT + 2)\n    hole.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x, y, -1))\n    ring_body = ring_body.cut(hole)\n\n    # Counterbore on bottom (for M5 socket head)\n    cb = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_CB_D / 2.0, BOLT_CB_H + 0.1)\n    cb.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x, y, -0.05))\n    ring_body = ring_body.cut(cb)\n\n# Outer edge chamfers\nif EDGE_CHAMFER > 0:\n    try:\n        edges_to_chamfer = []\n        for e in ring_body.Edges:\n            v1 = e.Vertexes[0].Point\n            v2 = e.Vertexes[1].Point\n            if abs(v1.z - v2.z) < 0.01:  # horizontal\n                r1 = (v1.x ** 2 + v1.y ** 2) ** 0.5\n                if abs(r1 - RING_OUTER_R) < 0.05:\n                    if abs(v1.z - 0) < 0.05 or abs(v1.z - RING_HEIGHT) < 0.05:\n                        edges_to_chamfer.append(e)\n        if edges_to_chamfer:\n            ring_body = ring_body.makeChamfer(EDGE_CHAMFER, edges_to_chamfer)\n            print(f\"  Chamfered {len(edges_to_chamfer)} outer edges\")\n    except Exception as ex:\n        print(f\"  Outer chamfer skipped: {ex}\")\n\nobj_ring = doc.addObject(\"Part::Feature\", \"CamRing_V6\")\nobj_ring.Shape = ring_body\nobj_ring.ViewObject.ShapeColor = (0.55, 0.45, 0.35)\nprint(f\"  Volume: {ring_body.Volume:.0f}mm^3 (~{ring_body.Volume * 1.27 / 1000:.0f}g PETG)\")\n\ndoc.recompute()\n\n# =============================================================================\n# EXPORT STL\n# =============================================================================\nprint(\"\\nExporting STL...\")\ntry:\n    Part.export([obj_ring], PATH_STL_FULL)\n    print(f\"  [OK] Cam Ring STL: {PATH_STL_FULL}\")\nexcept Exception as ex:\n    print(f\"  STL export error: {ex}\")\n\n#     # shallow cut at rest zone (butt sits in this much)\\nTRACK_DEPTH_PEAK = 9.0       # deep cut at peak (forces butt to lift)\\n# These translate to Z-position of needle:\\n# Rest depth 3mm -> butt    # slotted holes (locator + 5 orientators)\\n\\nBOLT_COUNT       = 6         # mount bolts to cassette base\\nBOLT_PCD         = 155.0\\nBOLT_HOLE_D      = 5.4       # M5 clearance\\nBOLT_CB_D        = 9.5       # counterbore\\nBOLT_CB_H        = 5.0\\n\\n# Yarn entry clearance (above ring, between needle hooks and feeder)\\n# This is just visual marker - actual yarn path is above VALUES\\n# =============================================================================\\nRING_OUTER_R     = RING_OD / 2.0\\nRING_INNER_R     = RING_ID / 2.0\\nTRACK_INNER_R_REST = RING_INNER_R +     {RING_HEIGHT}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Wall thickness:   {WALL_THICKNESS}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Cam track Z:      {TRACK_Z_BOT}-{TRACK_Z_TOP}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Cam profile:\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"    Rest depth:     {TRACK_DEPTH_REST}mm (315 deg of ring)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"    Peak depth:     {TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK}mm (5 deg, at ~295 deg)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"    Lift amount:    {TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK - TRACK_DEPTH_REST}mm needle travel\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Knit zone:        45 deg (rise + peak + fall)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Mount: {BOLT_COUNT}x M5 @ PCD {BOLT_PCD}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Pins:  {PIN_COUNT} alignment @ PCD {PIN_PCD}mm\\\")\\nprint()\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# DOCUMENT\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BUILD: BASE RING\\n# =============================================================================\\nprint(\\\"[1/3] Building base ring...\\\")\\n\\n# Hollow cylinder (outer minus inner)\\nouter_cyl = Part.makeCylinder(RING_OUTER_R, RING_HEIGHT)\\ninner_cyl = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R, RING_HEIGHT + 2)\\ninner_cyl.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\\nring_body = outer_cyl.cut(inner_cyl)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BUILD: CAM TRACK (the inner annular groove with V-cam profile)\\n# =============================================================================\\nprint(\\\"[2/3] Building cam track (V-cam profile)...\\\")\\n\\n# Strategy: cut the cam track as multiple boolean operations.\\n# Step 1: cut a uniform rest-depth track (3mm radial) all the way around\\n#         from Z=TRACK_Z_BOT to Z=TRACK_Z_TOP\\n# Step 2: cut deeper segments at the rise/peak/fall zones using rotated boxes\\n\\n# Step 1: Uniform rest track around the entire ring\\n# This is a flat annular cut at depth TRACK_DEPTH_REST\\nrest_outer = Part.makeCylinder(RING_OUTER_R + 1, TRACK_HEIGHT,\\n                               FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, TRACK_Z_BOT))\\nrest_inner_radius = RING_INNER_R  # don't cut anything inside the ID\\nrest_inner = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R, TRACK_HEIGHT + 0.1,\\n                              FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, TRACK_Z_BOT - 0.05))\\n# Actually we want to remove material from RING_INNER_R outward by TRACK_DEPTH_REST\\n# So the cut is between RING_INNER_R and RING_INNER_R + TRACK_DEPTH_REST\\nrest_track_cutter_outer = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R + TRACK_DEPTH_REST,\\n                                            TRACK_HEIGHT,\\n                                            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, TRACK_Z_BOT))\\nrest_track_cutter_inner = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R,\\n                                            TRACK_HEIGHT + 0.1,\\n                                            FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, TRACK_Z_BOT - 0.05))\\nrest_track_cutter = rest_track_cutter_outer.cut(rest_track_cutter_inner)\\nring_body = ring_body.cut(rest_track_cutter)\\nprint(f\\\"  Rest track cut at depth {TRACK_DEPTH_REST}mm, full circumference\\\")\\n\\n# Step 2: Deeper cuts at rise, peak, and fall zones\\n# We approximate the V-cam profile with 3 radial cuts that vary in depth.\\n# At the peak, we cut deeper into the wall (TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK).\\n# Rise and fall zones are at intermediate depth.\\n\\n# For simplicity in V1.0, use stepped depths:\\n# Rise zone (270-292.5): cut to depth 6mm (intermediate)\\n# Peak zone (292.5-297.5): cut to depth 9mm (max)\\n# Fall zone (297.5-315): cut to depth 6mm (intermediate)\\n\\ndef cut_angular_segment(ring, angle_start_deg, angle_end_deg, depth_mm,\\n                        z_bot, z_height):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Cut an angular segment of the inner wall to the specified depth.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    angle_span = angle_end_deg - angle_start_deg\\n    # Build a partial cylinder spanning the angular range\\n    # FreeCAD makeCylinder with angle parameter creates a sector\\n    sector = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R + depth_mm, z_height,\\n                              FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, z_bot),\\n                              FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\\n                              angle_span)\\n    # Rotate to start angle\\n    sector.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0),\\n                  FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\\n                  angle_start_deg)\\n    # Subtract the inner part (we don't want to cut beyond ID)\\n    inner_keep = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R, z_height + 0.1,\\n                                  FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, z_bot - 0.05))\\n    sector_cut = sector.cut(inner_keep)\\n    return ring.cut(sector_cut)\\n\\n# Apply rise zone (intermediate depth)\\nring_body = cut_angular_segment(ring_body,\\n                                ANGLE_REST_END, ANGLE_RISE_END,\\n                                (TRACK_DEPTH_REST + TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK) / 2.0,\\n                                TRACK_Z_BOT, TRACK_HEIGHT)\\nprint(f\\\"  Rise zone: {ANGLE_REST_END}-{ANGLE_RISE_END} deg, intermediate depth\\\")\\n\\n# Apply peak zone (max depth)\\nring_body = cut_angular_segment(ring_body,\\n                                ANGLE_RISE_END, ANGLE_PEAK_END,\\n                                TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK,\\n                                TRACK_Z_BOT, TRACK_HEIGHT)\\nprint(f\\\"  Peak zone: {ANGLE_RISE_END}-{ANGLE_PEAK_END} deg, max depth\\\")\\n\\n# Apply fall zone (intermediate depth)\\nring_body = cut_angular_segment(ring_body,\\n                                ANGLE_PEAK_END, ANGLE_FALL_END,\\n                                (TRACK_DEPTH_REST + TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK) / 2.0,\\n                                TRACK_Z_BOT, TRACK_HEIGHT)\\nprint(f\\\"  Fall zone: {ANGLE_PEAK_END}-{ANGLE_FALL_END} deg, intermediate depth\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BUILD: ALIGNMENT PIN HOLES\\n# =============================================================================\\nprint(\\\"[3/3] Cutting pin and bolt holes...\\\")\\n\\n# 6 pin holes at PCD 145mm (60 deg spacing)\\n# Hole 1 (0 deg): round locator\\n# Holes 2-6: slotted (tangential slots for thermal/print compensation)\\nfor i in range(PIN_COUNT):\\n    angle = i * (360.0 / PIN_COUNT)\\n    rad = PIN_PCD / 2.0\\n    x = rad * math.cos(math.radians(angle))\\n    y = rad * math.sin(math.radians(angle))\\n\\n    if i == 0:\\n        # Locator: round hole\\n        hole = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, RING_HEIGHT + 2)\\n        hole.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x, y, -1))\\n    else:\\n        # Orientator: slotted hole (tangential extension)\\n        # Build by fusing two holes offset tangentially\\n        slot_offset = (PIN_SLOT_LEN - PIN_HOLE_D) / 2.0\\n        # Tangential direction at this angle\\n        tan_x = -math.sin(math.radians(angle)) * slot_offset\\n        tan_y =  math.cos(math.radians(angle)) * slot_offset\\n\\n        hole_a = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, RING_HEIGHT + 2)\\n        hole_a.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x + tan_x, y + tan_y, -1))\\n\\n        hole_b = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, RING_HEIGHT + 2)\\n        hole_b.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x - tan_x, y - tan_y, -1))\\n\\n        # Connect with rectangular section\\n        hole_a = hole_a.fuse(hole_b)\\n        hole = hole_a\\n\\n    ring_body = ring_body.cut(hole)\\n\\n# 6 bolt holes at PCD 155mm (60 deg spacing, between pins)\\nfor i in range(BOLT_COUNT):\\n    angle = i * (360.0 / BOLT_COUNT) + 30.0  # offset 30 deg between pins\\n    rad = BOLT_PCD / 2.0\\n    x = rad * math.cos(math.radians(angle))\\n    y = rad * math.sin(math.radians(angle))\\n\\n    # M5 clearance hole\\n    hole = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_HOLE_D / 2.0, RING_HEIGHT + 2)\\n    hole.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x, y, -1))\\n    ring_body = ring_body.cut(hole)\\n\\n    # Counterbore on bottom (for M5 socket head)\\n    cb = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_CB_D / 2.0, BOLT_CB_H + 0.1)\\n    cb.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x, y, -0.05))\\n    ring_body = ring_body.cut(cb)\\n\\n# Outer edge chamfers\\nif EDGE_CHAMFER > 0:\\n    try:\\n        edges_to_chamfer = []\\n        for e in ring_body.Edges:\\n            v1 = e.Vertexes[0].Point\\n            v2 = e.Vertexes[1].Point\\n            if abs(v1.z - v2.z) < 0.01:  # horizontal\\n                r1 = (v1.x ** 2 + v1.y ** 2) ** 0.5\\n                if abs(r1 - RING_OUTER_R) < 0.05:\\n                    if abs(v1.z - 0) < 0.05 or abs(v1.z - RING_HEIGHT) < 0.05:\\n                        edges_to_chamfer.append(e)\\n        if edges_to_chamfer:\\n            ring_body = ring_body.makeChamfer(EDGE_CHAMFER, edges_to_chamfer)\\n            print(f\\\"  Chamfered {len(edges_to_chamfer)} outer edges\\\")\\n    except Exception as ex:\\n        print(f\\\"  Outer chamfer skipped: {ex}\\\")\\n\\nobj_ring = doc.addObject(\\\"Part::Feature\\\", \\\"CamRing_V6\\\")\\nobj_ring.Shape = ring_body\\nobj_ring.ViewObject.ShapeColor = (0.55, 0.45, 0.35)\\nprint(f\\\"  Volume: {ring_body.Volume:.0f}mm^3 (~{ring_body.Volume * 1.27 / 1000:.0f}g PETG)\\\")\\n\\ndoc.recompute()\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# EXPORT STL\\n# =============================================================================\\nprint(\\\"\\\\nExporting STL...\\\")\\ntry:\\n    Part.export([obj_ring], PATH_STL_FULL)\\n    print(f\\\"  [OK] Cam Ring STL: {PATH_STL_FULL}\\\")\\nexcept Exception as ex:\\n    print(f\\\"  STL export error: {ex}\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# EXPORT BLUEPRINT SVG\\n# =============================================================================\\nprint(\\\"\\\\nExporting blueprint...\\\")\\nsvg = f'''<?xml version=\\\"1.0\\\" encoding=\\\"UTF-8\\\"?>\\n<svg xmlns=\\\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\\\" width=\\\"900\\\" height=\\\"700\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 900 700\\\">\\n  <style>\\n    .title {{ font: bold 16px sans-serif; fill: #1e3a5f; }}\\n    .label {{ font: 10px sans-serif; fill: #333; }}\\n    .dim   {{ font: 11px sans-serif; fill: #b91c1c; }}\\n    .note  {{ font: 9px sans-serif; fill: #666; font-style: italic; }}\\n    .body  {{ fill: #d4cdb8; stroke: #1e3a5f; stroke-width: 1.5; }}\\n    .track {{ fill: #fff; stroke: #5a8d3e; stroke-width: 1; }}\\n    .peak  {{ fill: #fee; stroke: #b91c1c; stroke-width: 1; }}\\n  </style>\\n\\n  <text x=\\\"450\\\" y=\\\"30\\\" text-anchor=\\\"middle\\\" class=\\\"title\\\">CSM V3 Cam Ring V6 - Top View</text>\\n\\n  <!-- Top view -->\\n  <g transform=\\\"translate(280, The cylinder rotates inside it.\n  As needle butts (extending outward through cylinder wall) rotate\n  past the cam track, the V-cam profile lifts and lowers needles.\n  This is what creates the knitting motion.\n\nCAM PROFILE (V-cam, single knit point):\n  REST zone: 270 deg (most of ring) - needles stay low\n  RISE zone: 22.5 deg - smooth ramp up\n  PEAK zone: 5 deg - needles at maximum height (yarn enters hooks)\n  FALL zone: 17.5 deg - smooth ramp down (latch closes, stitch forms)\n  Total knit zone: 45 deg\n\nNEEDLE LIFT: 6mm (standard for sock knitting)\n\nDIMENSIONS:\n  Ring OD:        165mm\n  Ring ID:        115mm\n  Ring height:    25mm\n  Cam track Z:    4-19mm (15mm tall)\n  Track depth:    3mm rest, 9mm peak (= 6mm needle lift)\n\nMOUNTING:\n  6x M5 bolts to cassette base @ PCD 155mm\n  6x 4mm dowel pins for alignment @ PCD 145mm\n    1 round (locator) + 5 slotted (orientator + thermal compensation)\n\nMULTI-FEEDER STRATEGY:\n  Single cam profile means ONE knit point per rotation.\n  6 feeders mount above at F1 (0deg), F2 (60), F3 (120), F4 (180),\n  F5 (240), F6 (300).\n  Only ONE feeder is ACTIVE at any time.\n  Software switches feeders for color/pattern changes.\n  Initial 2-feeder build: F1 + F4 (180deg apart).\n\n==========================================\nHONEST CAVEAT - CAM PROFILE IS UNCERTAIN\n==========================================\n\nThe cam profile (rise/peak/fall geometry, lift amount, timing) is\nthe most uncertain part of the whole design. CSMs are notoriously\nsensitive to cam profile:\n  - Too aggressive lift -> dropped stitches\n  - Too gentle lift -> needle doesn't clear loop\n  - Wrong timing -> yarn misses hook\n  - Sharp transitions -> binding/wear\n\nV6 uses CONSERVATIVE defaults based on Legare/AutoKnitter precedent:\n  - 6mm lift (standard for 12g needles)\n  - 45deg total knit zone (typical)\n  - V-cam profile (single knit point)\n\nEXPECT iteration: after first knit test, you'll likely need V7 with\nrefined profile. This is NORMAL - even commercial CSMs are tuned\nthrough trial.\n\n==========================================\nPRINT INSTRUCTIONS\n==========================================\nMaterial:    PETG (DRY filament)\nLayer:       0.2mm\nPerimeters:  5\nTop/bottom:  6 layers\nInfill:      40-50% Cubic\nOrientation: ring lying flat (axis Z up)\nSupports:    NONE\nTime:        4-6 hours\nMaterial:    ~120-150g PETG\n\n==========================================\nASSEMBLY ORDER\n==========================================\n1. Print cam ring V6\n2. Cylinder V1.1.1 must be printed and assembled first\n3. Cassette base V1.0 should be printed first (mounts cam ring)\n4. Apply Loctite 603 to M5 bolts\n5. Insert 6 dowel pins into cassette base alignment holes\n6. Lower cam ring onto pins (round pin first, then slotted)\n7. Tighten 6 M5 bolts in cross pattern\n8. Cure Loctite 24h\n9. Hand-rotate cylinder, observe needle butt travel through cam track\n10. Verify smooth motion, no binding\n\n==========================================\nTESTING / TUNING PROTOCOL\n==========================================\n\nPhase 1 - Hand rotation test:\n  Without yarn, slowly rotate cylinder by hand.\n  Watch needle butts travel along cam track.\n  Look for:\n    - Smooth motion (no binding)\n    - Full lift achieved at peak\n    - Smooth descent (no abrupt drops)\n    - All 72 needles behave the same\n\nPhase 2 - Tuning if needed:\n  If needles bind: cam profile too aggressive\n    Reduce TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK (try 8mm)\n  If needles don't lift enough: profile too shallow\n    Increase TRACK_DEPTH_PEAK (try 10mm)\n  If needles drop sharply: fall zone too short\n    Increase ANGLE_FALL_END (try 320 deg)\n  If yarn misses hooks: timing wrong\n    Adjust ANGLE_PEAK_END relative to feeder positions\n\nPhase 3 - First knit test:\n  With ONE feeder active and yarn loaded:\n  Slowly rotate cylinder, observe stitch formation.\n  Document what works/fails.\n  Iterate to V7 if needed.\n\n==========================================\nHARDWARE NEEDED\n==========================================\n\nAlready have:\n  - PETG filament\n  - 6x M5 bolts (via screw assortment kit B0FFSNFK3Y)\n  - 6x 4mm dowel pins (B0FDWVGMQ9 or B0F5HFFVP7)\n  - Loctite 603 (B0074NALBO)\n\nNothing new to order for this part.\n\n==========================================\nNEXT STEPS\n==========================================\n\nPhase 1 (this delivery): Cam Ring V6 - DONE\nPhase 2 (next): Sinker Ring V1.0\nPhase 3 (next): Retainer Ring V1.0 (PA12)\nPhase 4 (next): Cassette Base V1.0\nPhase 5 (later): Feeder Module V1.0\nPhase 6 (later): Yarn Mast V1.0\n\nAfter all parts designed: print and assemble for first knit test.\nEOF\n\ncd csm_bom_camring\nzip -r /mnt/user-data/outputs/CSM_V3_BOM_V10_and_CamRing_V6.zip 3D-Project -q\n\necho \"=== ZIP CONTENTS ===\"\nunzip -l /mnt/user-data/outputs/CSM_V3_BOM_V10_and_CamRing_V6.zip\nls -lh /mnt/user-data/outputs/CSM_V3_BOM_V10_and_CamRing_V6.zip",

Define cam profile as 2D curve (theta, z)\\n  - Sweep profile along cylindrical inner surface\\n  - Generates true 3D smooth cam track\\n  - Hardest to implement in FreeCAD\\n\\nOPTION B: Stacked angular segments (approximation)\\n  - Slice Z range into thin layers\\n  - Each layer has different angular extent of CUT\\n  - Approximates ramp as staircase\\n  - Easier to implement but lower fidelity\\n\\nOPTION C: Wedge-shaped cuts (simple)\\n  - Cut wedges that form the rise/peak/fall directly\\n  - Each wedge has specific Z bottom and angular extent\\n  - Visually obvious geometry\\n  - Best balance of simplicity and correctness\\n\\nRECOMMENDATION: Option C\\n\\nWhy Option C:\\n  - {Z_PEAK}mm (cam bottom at peak)\")\nprint(f\"  Lift amount:      {LIFT}mm (V6.1: was 6mm - corrected)\")\nprint(f\"  Knit zone:        {ANGLE_FALL_END - ANGLE_RISE_START}deg\")\nprint()\n\n# =============================================================================\n# CAM PROFILE FUNCTION\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef cam_z_bot(theta_deg):\n    \"\"\"Returns Z bottom of cam slot at given angle (in degrees).\"\"\"\n    if theta_deg < ANGLE_RISE_START:\n        return Z_REST\n    elif theta_deg < ANGLE_RISE_END:\n        # Linear rise from Z_REST to Z_PEAK\n        rise_progress = (theta_deg - ANGLE_RISE_START) / (ANGLE_RISE_END - ANGLE_RISE_START)\n        return Z_REST + LIFT * rise_progress\n    elif theta_deg < ANGLE_PEAK_END:\n        # Hold at peak\n        return Z_PEAK\n    elif theta_deg < ANGLE_FALL_END:\n        # Linear fall from Z_PEAK back to Z_REST\n        fall_progress = (theta_deg - ANGLE_PEAK_END) / (ANGLE_FALL_END - ANGLE_PEAK_END)\n        return Z_PEAK - LIFT * fall_progress\n    else:\n        return Z_REST\n\n# \n    # Step 1: Build hollow ring (or partial sector)\n    if is_partial:\n        # Partial ring sector\n        outer = Part.makeCylinder(RING_OUTER_R, RING_HEIGHT,\n inner.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\n        ring = outer.cut(inner)\n    \n    # Step 2: Cut the cam slot using angular segments\n    # Each segment cuts a thin (1deg) angular slice with  # 1 degree per segment\n    \n    for i in range(seg_start, seg_end):\n        theta = i + 0.5  # midpoint of segment\n        z_bot = cam_z_bot(theta)\n        z_top = z_bot + SLOT_HEIGHT\n        \n        # Build a 1deg sector cutter at this Z range\n        # Outer radius slightly beyond ring OD (clean cut)\n        # Inner radius = SLOT_INNER_R (cut depth into wall)\n        # Need to cut FROM ID OUTWARD by SLOT_DEPTH\n        seg_outer = Part.makeCylinder(SLOT_INNER_R, SLOT_HEIGHT,\n                                      FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, z_bot),\n                                      FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\n                                      seg_size)\n        seg_outer.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0),\n                        FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1),\n                        i)\n        \n        seg_inner = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R, SLOT_HEIGHT + 0.1,\n                                      FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, z_bot - 0.05),\n                                      FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\n                                      seg_size + 0.05)\n        seg_inner.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0),\n                        FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1),\n                        i - 0.025)\n        \n        seg_cutter = seg_outer.cut(seg_inner)\n        cutters.append(seg_cutter)\n    \n    # Fuse all cutters and apply once (faster than 360 individual cuts)\n    if cutters:\n        all_cutters = cutters[0]\n        for c in cutters[1:]:\n            all_cutters = all_cutters.fuse(c)\n        ring = ring.cut(all_cutters)\n    \n    return ring\n\n\nprint(\"[1/2] Building FULL cam ring...\")\nring_full = build_cam_ring(0, 360, \"FullRing\")\n\n# Cut alignment pin holes\nprint(\"  Cutting alignment pin holes...\")\nfor i in range(PIN_COUNT):\n    angle = i * Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_HOLE_D / 2.0, RING_HEIGHT + 2)\n    hole.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x, y, -1))\n    ring_full = ring_full.cut(hole)\n\n    cb = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_CB_D / 2.0, BOLT_CB_H + 0.1)\n    cb.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x, y, -0.05))\n    ring_full = ring_full.cut(cb)\n\n# Outer chamfer\nif EDGE_CHAMFER > 0:\n    try:\n        edges_to_chamfer = []\n        for e in    if abs(r1 - RING_OUTER_R) < 0.05:\n                    if abs(v1.z - 0) < 0.05 or abs(v1.z - RING_HEIGHT) < 0.05:\n                        edges_to_chamfer.append(e)\n        if edges_to_chamfer:\n            ring_full = ring_full.makeChamfer(EDGE_CHAMFER, edges_to_chamfer)\n    except Exception as ex:\n        print(f\"  Outer chamfer skipped: {ex}\")\n\nobj_full = doc.addObject(\"Part::Feature\", \"CamRing_V6_1_FULL\")\nobj_full.Shape = ring_full\nobj_full.ViewObject.ShapeColor = (0.55, 0.45, 0.35)\nprint(f\"  Full ring volume: It used RADIAL depth variation to \\\"lift\\\" needles.\\n#   That doesn't work - radial motion doesn't change Z position of needle.\\n#\\n#   V6.1 uses CORRECT AXIAL Z profile:\\n#     - Cam track is a SLOT in inner wall\\n#     - Slot's bottom edge varies in Z\\n#     - Needle butt rides on this Z-varying edge\\n#     - As cylinder rotates, butt physically moves UP and DOWN\\n#\\n# OTHER CHANGES:\\n#   - Lift increased from 6mm to 10mm (per reviewer + standard practice)\\n#   - Cam profile defined as z(theta), not r(theta)\\n#\\n# DESIGN INTENT:\\n#   Stationary cam ring around rotating cylinder.\\n#   Needle butts extending outward through cylinder wall ride along\\n#   a Z-varying ramp on the cam ring's inner surface.\\n#   This ramp lifts needles to clear loops, then drops them to form stitches.\\n#\\n# CAM TRACK GEOMETRY:\\n#   - Track is an annular SLOT cut into ring's inner surface\\n#   - Slot height (axial): butt vertical + clearance = 4.5mm\\n#   - Slot depth (radial): ~3mm (gives butt enough clearance radially)\\n#   - Slot Z bottom edge: VARIES by angle (THIS IS THE CAM PROFILE)\\n#   - Slot Z top edge:    VARIES by angle (parallel, offset by slot Approximate the ramp as many small angular segments.\\n#   Each segment is a thin angular slice with constant Z bottom.\\n#   Step size of 1 degree gives smooth-enough profile for V1.\\n# =============================================================================\\n# CAM PROFILE FUNCTION\\n# =============================================================================\\n\\ndef cam_z_bot(theta_deg):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Returns Z bottom of cam slot at given angle (in degrees).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if theta_deg < ANGLE_RISE_START:\\n        return Z_REST\\n    elif theta_deg < \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    is_partial = (end_angle_deg - start_angle_deg) < 359.0\\n    angular_extent = end_angle_deg - start_angle_deg\\n    \\n    print(f\\\"  Building {name}: angles   # Determine angular range for this build\\n    if is_partial:\\n        seg_start = int(start_angle_deg)\\n        seg_end = int(end_angle_deg)\\n    else:\\n        seg_start = 0\\n        seg_end = 360\\n    \\n    # Build all segments and accumulate\\n    cutters = []\\n    seg_size = 1.0  # 1 degree per segment\\n    \\n    for i in range(seg_start, seg_end):\\n        theta = i + 0.5  # SLOT_HEIGHT,\\n                                      FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, z_bot),\\n                                      FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\\n                                      seg_size)\\n        seg_outer.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0),\\n                        FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1),\\n                        i)\\n        \\n        seg_inner = Part.makeCylinder(RING_INNER_R, SLOT_HEIGHT + 0.1,\\n                                      FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, z_bot - 0.05),\\n                                      FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\\n                                      seg_size + 0.05)\\n        seg_inner.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0),\\n                        FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1),\\n                        i - 0.025)\\n        \\n        seg_cutter = seg_outer.cut(seg_inner)\\n        cutters.append(seg_cutter)\\n    \\n    # Fuse all cutters and apply once (faster than 360 individual cuts)\\n    if cutters:\\n        all_cutters = cutters[0]\\n        for c in cutters[1:]:\\n            all_cutters = all_cutters.fuse(c)\\n        ring = ring.cut(all_cutters)\\n    \\n    return ring\\n\\n\\nprint(\\\"[1/2] Building FULL cam Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, RING_HEIGHT + 2)\\n        hole_a.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x + tan_x, y + tan_y, -1))\\n        hole_b = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, RING_HEIGHT + 2)\\n        hole_b.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x - tan_x, y - tan_y, -1))\\n        hole = hole_a.fuse(hole_b)\\n\\n    ring_full = ring_full.cut(hole)\\n\\n# Cut bolt holes with counterbores\\nprint(\\\"  Cutting bolt holes...\\\")\\nfor i in range(BOLT_COUNT):\\n    angle = i * (360.0 / BOLT_COUNT) + 30.0\\n    rad = BOLT_PCD / 2.0\\n    x = rad * math.cos(math.radians(angle))\\n    y = rad * math.sin(math.radians(angle))\\n\\n    hole = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_HOLE_D / 2.0, RING_HEIGHT + 2)\\n    hole.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x, y, -1))\\n    ring_full = ring_full.cut(hole)\\n\\n    cb = (v1.x ** 2 + v1.y ** 2) ** 0.5\\n                if abs(r1 - RING_OUTER_R) < 0.05:\\n                    if abs(v1.z - 0) < 0.05 or abs(v1.z - RING_HEIGHT) < 0.05:\\n                        edges_to_chamfer.append(e)\\n        if edges_to_chamfer:\\n            ring_full = ring_full.makeChamfer(EDGE_CHAMFER, edges_to_chamfer)\\n    except Exception as ex:\\n        print(f\\\"  Outer 45 degrees)\\n\\nTotal knit zone: 45 degrees (12.5% of rotation)\\n\\n==========================================\\nDIMENSIONS\\n==========================================\\n\\nRing:\\n  OD:               165mm\\n  ID:               115mm (clears 114.3mm cylinder + 0.7mm)\\n  Height:           35mm (V6.1: increased from 25mm to fit full lift)\\n  Wall thickness:   25mm\\n\\nCam slot:\\n  Slot height:      4.5mm axial (fits 3.64mm butt + 0.86mm clearance)\\n  Slot depth:       3.0mm radial (butt extends 3mm into ring)\\n  Z rest (bottom):  5mm\\n  Z peak (bottom):  15mm\\n  Lift amount:      10mm (V6.1: was 6mm in V6 - logic\n\n==========================================\nWHY EACH CHANGE MATTERS\n==========================================\n\nSLOT_HEIGHT 4.5 -> 6.0:\n  Butt is 3.64mm tall.\n  At V6.1: 4.5 - 3.64 = 0.86mm total clearance (0.43mm/side)\n  PETG print tolerance: ~0.2mm\n  Worst case clearance: 0.46mm - tight, especially at transitions\n  \n  At V6.2: 6.0 - 3.64 = 2.36mm clearance (1.18mm/side)\n  Worst case: 0.98mm - comfortable, robust to print variation\n\nPROFILE_STEPS 360 -> 720:\n  Slope: 10mm lift / 22.5deg rise = 0.444mm per degree\n  At 1deg steps: each segment is 0.44mm taller than previous\n  At 0.2mm PETG layer height, each step spans 2+ layers = visible stairs\n  \n  At 0.5deg steps: each segment is 0.22mm taller\n  Now matches PETG layer height = slicer blends smoothly\n  No staircase artifacts on cam surface\n\nRise 22.5 -> 30deg (gentler slope):\n  Steeper rise = faster needle motion = more stress on butt-cam contact\n  Gentler rise = smoother operation, less wear, less vibration\n  Slope: 0.444 -> 0.333 mm/deg (25% gentler)\n  \n  Real CSMs: typical rise is 25-35deg\n  V6.1 (22.5) was on aggressive end\n  V6.2 (30) is more typical\n\nZ_OFFSET tuning param:\n  If first print needs Z shift (e.g. cam too high or too low),\n  just change Z_OFFSET, regenerate, reprint\n  Don't need to touch the profile logic\n\n==========================================\nDIMENSIONS (V6.2)\n==========================================\n\nRing:\n  OD:               165mm\n  ID:               115mm (clears 114.3mm
